We only use the temperature. While this\n   386→    # could degrade the acceptance rate, it does not affect the distribution\n   387→    # of the generated tokens after rejection sampling.\n   388→\n   389→    # TODO(woosuk): Consider seeds.\n   390→    q = torch.empty_like(probs)\n   391→    q.exponential_()\n   392→    # NOTE(woosuk): We shouldn't use `probs.div_(q)` because the draft_probs\n   393→    # will be used later for rejection sampling.\n   394→    next_token_ids = probs.div(q).argmax(dim=-1).view(-1)\n   395→    if not sampling_metadata.all_random:\n   396→        greedy_token_ids = probs.argmax(dim=-1)\n   397→        next_token_ids = torch.where(\n   398→            is_greedy,\n   399→            greedy_token_ids,\n   400→            next_token_ids,\n   401→        )\n   402→    return next_token_ids, probs\n   403→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. get_layers_from_vllm_config)\nfrom vllm.distributed.parallel_state import get_pp_group\nfrom vllm.forward_context import set_forward_context\nfrom vllm.logger import init_logger\nfrom vllm.model_executor.model_loader import get_model\nfrom vllm.model_executor.models.llama_eagle3 import Eagle3LlamaForCausalLM\nfrom vllm.v1.attention.backends.flash_attn import (CommonAttentionMetadata,\n                                                   FlashAttentionMetadata)\nfrom vllm.v1.sample.metadata import SamplingMetadata\nfrom vllm.v1.spec_decode.utils import prepare_eagle_input_kernel\n\nlogger = init_logger(__name__)\n\nPADDING_SLOT_ID = -1\n\n\nclass EagleProposer:\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        vllm_config: VllmConfig,\n        device: torch.device,\n        runner=None,\n    ):\n        self.vllm_config = vllm_config\n        self.speculative_config = vllm_config.speculative_config\n        self.draft_model_config = self.speculative_config.draft_model_config\n        self.method = self.speculative_config.method\n\n        self.runner = runner\n\n        self.dtype = vllm_config.model_config.dtype\n        self.max_model_len = vllm_config.model_config.max_model_len\n        self.block_size = vllm_config.cache_config.block_size\n        self.num_speculative_tokens = (\n            self.speculative_config.num_speculative_tokens)\n        self.max_num_tokens = (\n            vllm_config.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens)\n        # We need to get the hidden size from the draft model config because\n        # the draft model's hidden size can be different from the target model's\n        # hidden size (e.g., Llama 3.3 70B).\n        self.hidden_size = self.draft_model_config.get_hidden_size()\n\n        self.use_cuda_graph = (self.vllm_config.compilation_config.level\n                               == CompilationLevel.PIECEWISE and\n                               not self.vllm_config.model_config.enforce_eager)\n        self.cudagraph_batch_sizes = list(\n            reversed(\n                self.vllm_config.compilation_config.cudagraph_capture_sizes))\n\n        # persistent buffers for cuda graph\n        self.input_ids = torch.zeros(self.max_num_tokens,\n                                     dtype=torch.int32,\n                                     device=device)\n        self.positions = torch.zeros(self.max_num_tokens,\n                                     dtype=torch.int64,\n                                     device=device)\n        self.hidden_states = torch.zeros(\n            (self.max_num_tokens, self.hidden_size),\n            dtype=self.dtype,\n            device=device)\n        # We need +1 here because the arange is used to set query_start_loc,\n        # which has one more element than batch_size.\n        self.arange = torch.arange(vllm_config.scheduler_config.max_num_seqs +\n                                   1,\n                                   device=device,\n                                   dtype=torch.int32)\n\n    def propose(\n        self,\n        # [num_tokens]\n        target_token_ids: torch.Tensor,\n        # [num_tokens]\n        target_positions: torch.Tensor,\n        # [num_tokens, hidden_size]\n        target_hidden_states: torch.Tensor,\n        # [num_tokens]\n        target_slot_mapping: torch.Tensor,\n        # [batch_size]\n        next_token_ids: torch.Tensor,\n        # [batch_size + 1] starting with 0\n        cu_num_tokens: torch.Tensor,\n        # [batch_size, max_num_blocks_per_req]\n        block_table: torch.Tensor,\n        sampling_metadata: SamplingMetadata,\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        num_tokens = target_token_ids.shape[0]\n        batch_size = next_token_ids.shape[0]\n        last_token_indices = cu_num_tokens[1:] - 1\n\n        if self.method == \"eagle3\":\n            assert isinstance(self.model, Eagle3LlamaForCausalLM)\n            target_hidden_states = self.model.combine_hidden_states(\n                target_hidden_states)\n            assert target_hidden_states.shape[-1] == self.hidden_size\n\n        # Shift the input ids by one token.\n        # E.g., [a1, b1, b2, c1, c2, c3] -> [b1, b2, c1, c2, c3, c3]\n        self.input_ids[:num_tokens - 1] = target_token_ids[1:]\n        # Replace the last token with the next token.\n        # E.g., [b1, b2, c1, c2, c3, c3] -> [a2, b2, b3, c2, c3, c4]\n        self.input_ids[last_token_indices] = next_token_ids\n\n        # FA requires seq_len to have dtype int32.\n        seq_lens = (target_positions[last_token_indices] + 1).int()\n\n        if self.method in [\"eagle\", \"eagle3\"]:\n            # FIXME(woosuk): The below two ops cause synchronization. Optimize.\n            max_seq_len = seq_lens.max().item()\n            max_num_tokens = (cu_num_tokens[1:] -\n                              cu_num_tokens[:-1]).max().item()\n            attn_metadata = FlashAttentionMetadata(\n                num_actual_tokens=num_tokens,\n                max_query_len=max_num_tokens,\n                query_start_loc=cu_num_tokens,\n                max_seq_len=max_seq_len,\n                seq_lens=seq_lens,\n                block_table=block_table,\n                slot_mapping=target_slot_mapping,\n                # TODO(woosuk): Support cascade attention.\n                use_cascade=False,\n                common_prefix_len=0,\n                cu_prefix_query_lens=None,\n                prefix_kv_lens=None,\n                suffix_kv_lens=None,\n            )\n        elif self.method == \"deepseek_mtp\":\n            query_lens = cu_num_tokens[1:] - cu_num_tokens[:-1]\n            max_query_len = query_lens.max().item()\n\n            common_attn_metadata = CommonAttentionMetadata(\n                query_start_loc=cu_num_tokens, seq_lens=seq_lens)\n\n            assert self.runner is not None\n\n            # FIXME: need to consider multiple kv_cache_groups\n            attn_metadata = self.runner.attn_metadata_builder.build(\n                num_reqs=batch_size,\n                num_actual_tokens=num_tokens,\n                max_query_len=max_query_len,\n                common_prefix_len=0,\n                common_attn_metadata=common_attn_metadata,\n            )\n        else:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Unsupported method: {self.method}\")\n\n        if self.use_cuda_graph and \\\n            num_tokens <= self.cudagraph_batch_sizes[-1]:\n            num_input_tokens = self.vllm_config.pad_for_cudagraph(num_tokens)\n        else:\n            num_input_tokens = num_tokens\n        # copy inputs to buffer for cudagraph\n        self.positions[:num_tokens] = target_positions\n        self.hidden_states[:num_tokens] = target_hidden_states\n\n        with set_forward_context(attn_metadata,\n                                 self.vllm_config,\n                                 num_tokens=num_input_tokens):\n            ret_hidden_states = self.model(\n                self.input_ids[:num_input_tokens],\n                self.positions[:num_input_tokens],\n                self.hidden_states[:num_input_tokens],\n            )\n            if self.method == \"deepseek_mtp\":\n                last_hidden_states = ret_hidden_states\n            else:\n                last_hidden_states, hidden_states = ret_hidden_states\n        sample_hidden_states = last_hidden_states[last_token_indices]\n        logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states, None)\n        draft_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)\n\n        # Early exit if there is only one draft token to be generated.\n        if self.num_speculative_tokens == 1:\n            # [batch_size, 1]\n            return draft_token_ids.view(-1, 1)\n\n        # TODO: Currently, MTP module released by deepseek only has\n        # one layer. Adapt this code to support multiple layers once\n        # there's a multi-layer MTP module.\n\n        # Generate the remaining draft tokens.\n        draft_token_ids_list = [draft_token_ids]\n\n        positions = target_positions[last_token_indices]\n        hidden_states = hidden_states[last_token_indices]\n        if self.use_cuda_graph and \\\n            batch_size <= self.cudagraph_batch_sizes[-1]:\n            input_batch_size = self.vllm_config.pad_for_cudagraph(batch_size)\n        else:\n            input_batch_size = batch_size\n        attn_metadata.num_actual_tokens = batch_size\n        attn_metadata.max_query_len = 1\n        attn_metadata.query_start_loc = self.arange[:batch_size + 1]\n        for _ in range(self.num_speculative_tokens - 1):\n            # Update the inputs.\n            # cast to int32 is crucial when eagle model is compiled.\n            # tensor.argmax() returns int64 by default.\n            input_ids = draft_token_ids_list[-1].int()\n            positions += 1\n\n            # NOTE(woosuk): We should handle the case where the draft model\n            # generates tokens beyond the max model length. Since it is complex\n            # to remove such requests from the batch, we keep them in the batch\n            # but adjust the position ids and slot mappings to avoid the\n            # out-of-range access during the model execution. The draft tokens\n            # generated with this adjustment should be ignored.\n            exceeds_max_model_len = positions >= self.max_model_len\n            # Mask out the position ids that exceed the max model length.\n            # Otherwise, we may get out-of-range error in RoPE.\n            clamped_positions = torch.where(exceeds_max_model_len, 0,\n                                            positions)\n\n            # Increment the sequence lengths.\n            attn_metadata.max_seq_len += 1\n            attn_metadata.seq_lens += 1\n            # Consider max model length.\n            attn_metadata.max_seq_len = min(attn_metadata.max_seq_len,\n                                            self.max_model_len)\n            # For the requests that exceed the max model length, we set the\n            # sequence length to 1 to minimize their overheads in attention.\n            attn_metadata.seq_lens.masked_fill_(exceeds_max_model_len, 1)\n\n            # Compute the slot mapping.\n            block_numbers = clamped_positions // self.block_size\n            block_ids = block_table.gather(dim=1,\n                                           index=block_numbers.view(-1, 1))\n            block_ids = block_ids.view(-1)\n            attn_metadata.slot_mapping = (block_ids * self.block_size +\n                                          clamped_positions % self.block_size)\n            # Mask out the slot mappings that exceed the max model length.\n            # Otherwise, the KV cache will be inadvertently updated with the\n            # padding tokens.\n            attn_metadata.slot_mapping.masked_fill_(exceeds_max_model_len,\n                                                    PADDING_SLOT_ID)\n\n            # copy inputs to buffer for cudagraph\n            self.input_ids[:batch_size] = input_ids\n            self.positions[:batch_size] = clamped_positions\n            self.hidden_states[:batch_size] = hidden_states\n\n            # Run the model.\n            with set_forward_context(attn_metadata,\n                                     self.vllm_config,\n                                     num_tokens=input_batch_size):\n                last_hidden_states, hidden_states = self.model(\n                    self.input_ids[:input_batch_size],\n                    self.positions[:input_batch_size],\n                    self.hidden_states[:input_batch_size],\n                )\n            hidden_states = hidden_states[:batch_size]\n            logits = self.model.compute_logits(last_hidden_states[:batch_size],\n                                               None)\n\n            # TODO(wenlong): get more than one token for tree attention\n            draft_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)\n            draft_token_ids_list.append(draft_token_ids)\n\n        # [batch_size, num_speculative_tokens]\n        draft_token_ids = torch.stack(draft_token_ids_list, dim=1)\n        return draft_token_ids\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def prepare_inputs(\n        # [batch_size + 1]\n        cu_target_query_lens: torch.Tensor,\n        # [batch_size]\n        num_rejected_tokens: torch.Tensor,\n        num_tokens: int,\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n        # cu_target_query_lens: [0, a, a + b, a + b + c]\n        # num_rejected_tokens: [n1, n2, n3]\n        # num_tokens_per_req: [a - n1, b - n2, c - n3]\n        # cu_num_tokens: [0, a - n1, a + b - n1 - n2, a + b + c - n1 - n2 - n3]\n        # token_indices: [0, 1, ..., a - n1 - 1,\n        #                 a, a + 1, ..., a + b - n2 - 1,\n        #                 a + b, a + b + 1, ..., a + b + c - n3 - 1]\n\n        # [0, a, a + b, a + b + c] -> [a, b, c]\n        query_len_per_req = (cu_target_query_lens[1:] -\n                             cu_target_query_lens[:-1])\n        # [a, b, c] -> [a - n1, b - n2, c - n3]\n        num_tokens_per_req = query_len_per_req - num_rejected_tokens\n\n        # [a - n1, b - n2, c - n3] ->\n        # [0, a - n1, a + b - n1 - n2, a + b + c - n1 - n2 - n3]\n        cu_num_tokens = torch.empty_like(cu_target_query_lens)\n        torch.cumsum(num_tokens_per_req, dim=0, out=cu_num_tokens[1:])\n        cu_num_tokens[0] = 0\n\n        token_indices = torch.empty(\n            num_tokens,\n            dtype=torch.int32,\n            device=cu_num_tokens.device,\n        )\n\n        batch_size = num_rejected_tokens.shape[0]\n        BLOCK_SIZE = 1024\n        prepare_eagle_input_kernel[(batch_size, )](\n            token_indices,\n            cu_target_query_lens,\n            cu_num_tokens,\n            BLOCK_SIZE=BLOCK_SIZE,\n        )\n        return cu_num_tokens, token_indices\n\n    def load_model(self, target_model: nn.Module) -> None:\n        draft_model_config = \\\n            self.vllm_config.speculative_config.draft_model_config\n        target_attn_layer_names = set(\n            get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, Attention).keys())\n\n        self.model = get_model(vllm_config=self.vllm_config,\n                               model_config=draft_model_config)\n\n        draft_attn_layer_names = (\n            get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, Attention).keys() -\n            target_attn_layer_names)\n        assert len(draft_attn_layer_names) == 1\n        self.attn_layer_name = next(iter(draft_attn_layer_names))\n\n        # share embed_tokens with the target model if needed\n        if get_pp_group().world_size == 1:\n            logger.info(\n                \"The EAGLE head shares the same vocab embedding\" \\\n                \" with the target model.\"\n            )\n            self.model.model.embed_tokens = target_model.model.embed_tokens\n        else:\n            logger.info(\n                \"Since PP > 1, the EAGLE head loaded its own vocab embedding\" \\\n                \" weights instead of sharing them with the target model.\"\n            )\n\n        # share lm_head with the target model if needed\n        # some model definition do not define lm_head explicitly\n        # and reuse embed_tokens for lm_head, e.g., CohereForCausalLM\n        if self.vllm_config.speculative_config.method != \"eagle3\" and \\\n                hasattr(target_model, \"lm_head\"):\n            logger.info(\"Loading EAGLE LM head weights from the target model.\")\n            self.model.lm_head = target_model.lm_head\n\n    @torch.inference_mode()\n    def dummy_run(\n        self,\n        num_tokens: int,\n    ) -> None:\n        with set_forward_context(None, self.vllm_config,\n                                 num_tokens=num_tokens):\n            self.model(\n                self.input_ids[:num_tokens],\n                self.positions[:num_tokens],\n                self.hidden_states[:num_tokens],\n            )\n\n\n# NOTE(woosuk): Currently, the below code is not used and we always use argmax\n# to sample the draft tokens. We will use this after we find a way to manage\n# the draft prob tensor.\n# Refer to https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/16899 for the details.\n# FIXME(woosuk): The logic here is duplicated with the main sampling code.\n# We should refactor this to reuse the same sampling implementation.\ndef compute_probs_and_sample_next_token(\n    logits: torch.Tensor,\n    sampling_metadata: SamplingMetadata,\n) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n    if sampling_metadata.all_greedy:\n        # For greedy requests, draft_probs is not used in rejection sampling.\n        # Therefore, we can just return the logits.\n        probs = logits\n        next_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)\n        return next_token_ids, probs\n\n    is_greedy = sampling_metadata.temperature == -1\n    temperature = torch.where(is_greedy, 1.0, sampling_metadata.temperature)\n    logits.div_(temperature.view(-1, 1))\n    probs = logits.softmax(dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32)\n\n    # NOTE(woosuk): Currently, we ignore most of the sampling parameters in\n    # generating the draft tokens. We only use the temperature. While this\n    # could degrade the acceptance rate, it does not affect the distribution\n    # of the generated tokens after rejection sampling.\n\n    # TODO(woosuk): Consider seeds.\n    q = torch.empty_like(probs)\n    q.exponential_()\n    # NOTE(woosuk): We shouldn't use `probs.div_(q)` because the draft_probs\n    # will be used later for rejection sampling.\n    next_token_ids = probs.div(q).argmax(dim=-1).view(-1)\n    if not sampling_metadata.all_random:\n        greedy_token_ids = probs.argmax(dim=-1)\n        next_token_ids = torch.where(\n            is_greedy,\n            greedy_token_ids,\n            next_token_ids,\n        )\n    return next_token_ids, probs\n","numLines":403,"startLine":1,"totalLines":403}}}

prepare_eagle_input_kernel\n\nlogger = init_logger(__name__)\n\nPADDING_SLOT_ID = -1\n\n\nclass EagleProposer:\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        vllm_config: VllmConfig,\n        device: self.speculative_config.draft_model_config\n        self.method = self.speculative_config.method\n\n        self.runner = runner\n\n        self.dtype = vllm_config.model_config.dtype\n        self.max_model_len = vllm_config.model_config.max_model_len\n        self.block_size = vllm_config.cache_config.block_size\n        self.num_speculative_tokens = (\n            self.speculative_config.num_speculative_tokens)\n        self.max_num_tokens = (\n            vllm_config.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens)\n        # We need to get the hidden size from the draft model config because\n        # the draft model's hidden size can be different from the target model's\n        # hidden size (e.g., Llama 3.3 70B).\n        self.hidden_size = self.draft_model_config.get_hidden_size()\n\n        self.use_cuda_graph = (self.vllm_config.compilation_config.level\n                               == CompilationLevel.PIECEWISE and\n                               not self.vllm_config.model_config.enforce_eager)\n        self.cudagraph_batch_sizes = list(\n            reversed(\n                self.vllm_config.compilation_config.cudagraph_capture_sizes))\n\n        # persistent buffers for cuda graph\n        self.input_ids = torch.zeros(self.max_num_tokens,\n                                     dtype=torch.int32,\n                                     device=device)\n        self.positions = torch.zeros(self.max_num_tokens,\n                                     dtype=torch.int64,\n                                     device=device)\n        self.hidden_states = torch.zeros(\n            (self.max_num_tokens, self.hidden_size),\n            dtype=self.dtype,\n            device=device)\n        # We need +1 here because the arange is used to set query_start_loc,\n        # which has one more element than batch_size.\n        self.arange = torch.arange(vllm_config.scheduler_config.max_num_seqs +\n                                   1,\n                                   device=device,\n                                   dtype=torch.int32)\n\n    def propose(\n        self,\n        # [num_tokens]\n        target_token_ids: torch.Tensor,\n        # [num_tokens]\n        target_positions: torch.Tensor,\n        # [num_tokens, hidden_size]\n        target_hidden_states: torch.Tensor,\n        # [num_tokens]\n        target_slot_mapping: torch.Tensor,\n        # [batch_size]\n        next_token_ids: torch.Tensor,\n        # [batch_size + 1] starting with 0\n        cu_num_tokens: torch.Tensor,\n        # [batch_size, max_num_blocks_per_req]\n        block_table: torch.Tensor,\n        sampling_metadata: SamplingMetadata,\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        num_tokens = target_token_ids.shape[0]\n        batch_size = next_token_ids.shape[0]\n        last_token_indices = cu_num_tokens[1:] - 1\n\n        if self.method == \"eagle3\":\n            assert isinstance(self.model, Eagle3LlamaForCausalLM)\n            target_hidden_states = self.model.combine_hidden_states(\n                target_hidden_states)\n            assert target_hidden_states.shape[-1] == self.hidden_size\n\n        # Shift the input ids by one token.\n        # E.g., [a1, b1, b2, c1, c2, c3] -> [b1, b2, c1, c2, c3, c3]\n        self.input_ids[:num_tokens - 1] = target_token_ids[1:]\n        # Replace the last token with the next token.\n        # E.g., [b1, b2, c1, c2, c3, c3] -> [a2, b2, b3, c2, c3, c4]\n        self.input_ids[last_token_indices] = next_token_ids\n\n        # FA requires seq_len to have dtype int32.\n        seq_lens = (target_positions[last_token_indices] + 1).int()\n\n        if self.method in [\"eagle\", \"eagle3\"]:\n            # FIXME(woosuk): The below two ops cause synchronization. Optimize.\n            max_seq_len = seq_lens.max().item()\n            max_num_tokens = (cu_num_tokens[1:] -\n                              cu_num_tokens[:-1]).max().item()\n            attn_metadata = FlashAttentionMetadata(\n                num_actual_tokens=num_tokens,\n                max_query_len=max_num_tokens,\n                query_start_loc=cu_num_tokens,\n                max_seq_len=max_seq_len,\n                seq_lens=seq_lens,\n                block_table=block_table,\n                slot_mapping=target_slot_mapping,\n                # TODO(woosuk): Support cascade attention.\n                use_cascade=False,\n                common_prefix_len=0,\n                cu_prefix_query_lens=None,\n                prefix_kv_lens=None,\n                suffix_kv_lens=None,\n            )\n        elif self.method == \"deepseek_mtp\":\n            query_lens = cu_num_tokens[1:] - cu_num_tokens[:-1]\n            max_query_len = query_lens.max().item()\n\n            common_attn_metadata = CommonAttentionMetadata(\n                query_start_loc=cu_num_tokens, seq_lens=seq_lens)\n\n            assert self.runner is not None\n\n            # FIXME: need to consider multiple kv_cache_groups\n            attn_metadata = self.runner.attn_metadata_builder.build(\n                num_reqs=batch_size,\n                num_actual_tokens=num_tokens,\n                max_query_len=max_query_len,\n                common_prefix_len=0,\n                common_attn_metadata=common_attn_metadata,\n            )\n        else:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Unsupported method: {self.method}\")\n\n        if self.use_cuda_graph and \\\n            num_tokens <= self.cudagraph_batch_sizes[-1]:\n            num_input_tokens = self.vllm_config.pad_for_cudagraph(num_tokens)\n        else:\n            num_input_tokens = num_tokens\n        # copy inputs to buffer for cudagraph\n        self.positions[:num_tokens] = target_positions\n        self.hidden_states[:num_tokens] = target_hidden_states\n\n        with set_forward_context(attn_metadata,\n                                 self.vllm_config,\n                                 num_tokens=num_input_tokens):\n            ret_hidden_states = self.model(\n                self.input_ids[:num_input_tokens],\n                self.positions[:num_input_tokens],\n                self.hidden_states[:num_input_tokens],\n            )\n            if self.method == \"deepseek_mtp\":\n                last_hidden_states = ret_hidden_states\n            else:\n                last_hidden_states, hidden_states = ret_hidden_states\n        sample_hidden_states = last_hidden_states[last_token_indices]\n        logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states, None)\n        draft_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)\n\n        # Early exit if there is only one draft token to be generated.\n        if self.num_speculative_tokens == 1:\n            # [batch_size, 1]\n            return draft_token_ids.view(-1, 1)\n\n        # TODO: Currently, MTP module released by deepseek only has\n        # one layer. Adapt this code to support multiple layers once\n        # there's a multi-layer MTP module.\n\n        # Generate the remaining draft tokens.\n        draft_token_ids_list = [draft_token_ids]\n\n        positions = target_positions[last_token_indices]\n        hidden_states = hidden_states[last_token_indices]\n        if self.use_cuda_graph and \\\n            batch_size <= self.cudagraph_batch_sizes[-1]:\n            input_batch_size = self.vllm_config.pad_for_cudagraph(batch_size)\n        else:\n            input_batch_size = batch_size\n        attn_metadata.num_actual_tokens = batch_size\n        attn_metadata.max_query_len = 1\n        attn_metadata.query_start_loc = self.arange[:batch_size + 1]\n        for _ in range(self.num_speculative_tokens - 1):\n            # Update the inputs.\n            # cast to int32 is crucial when eagle model is compiled.\n            # tensor.argmax() returns int64 by default.\n            input_ids = draft_token_ids_list[-1].int()\n            positions += 1\n\n            # NOTE(woosuk): We should handle the case where the draft model\n            # generates tokens beyond the max model length. Since it is complex\n            # to remove such requests from the batch, we keep them in the batch\n            # but adjust the position ids and slot mappings to avoid the\n            # out-of-range access during the model execution. The draft tokens\n            # generated with this adjustment should be ignored.\n            exceeds_max_model_len = positions >= self.max_model_len\n            # Mask out the position ids that exceed the max model length.\n            # Otherwise, we may get out-of-range error in RoPE.\n            clamped_positions = torch.where(exceeds_max_model_len, 0,\n                                            positions)\n\n            # Increment the sequence lengths.\n            attn_metadata.max_seq_len += 1\n            attn_metadata.seq_lens += 1\n            # Consider max model length.\n            attn_metadata.max_seq_len = min(attn_metadata.max_seq_len,\n                                            self.max_model_len)\n            # For the requests that exceed the max model length, we set the\n            # sequence length to 1 to minimize their overheads in attention.\n            attn_metadata.seq_lens.masked_fill_(exceeds_max_model_len, 1)\n\n            # Compute the slot mapping.\n            block_numbers = clamped_positions // self.block_size\n            block_ids = block_table.gather(dim=1,\n                                           index=block_numbers.view(-1, 1))\n            block_ids = block_ids.view(-1)\n            attn_metadata.slot_mapping = (block_ids * self.block_size +\n                                          clamped_positions % self.block_size)\n            # Mask out the slot mappings that exceed the max model length.\n            # Otherwise, the KV cache will be inadvertently updated with the\n            # padding tokens.\n            attn_metadata.slot_mapping.masked_fill_(exceeds_max_model_len,\n                                                    PADDING_SLOT_ID)\n\n            # copy inputs to buffer for cudagraph\n            self.input_ids[:batch_size] = input_ids\n            self.positions[:batch_size] = clamped_positions\n            self.hidden_states[:batch_size] = hidden_states\n\n            # Run the model.\n            with set_forward_context(attn_metadata,\n                                     self.vllm_config,\n                                     num_tokens=input_batch_size):\n                last_hidden_states, hidden_states = self.model(\n                    self.input_ids[:input_batch_size],\n                    self.positions[:input_batch_size],\n                    self.hidden_states[:input_batch_size],\n                )\n            hidden_states = hidden_states[:batch_size]\n            logits = self.model.compute_logits(last_hidden_states[:batch_size],\n                                               None)\n\n            # TODO(wenlong): get more than one token for tree attention\n            draft_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)\n            draft_token_ids_list.append(draft_token_ids)\n\n        # [batch_size, num_speculative_tokens]\n        draft_token_ids = torch.stack(draft_token_ids_list, dim=1)\n        return draft_token_ids\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def prepare_inputs(\n        # [batch_size + 1]\n        cu_target_query_lens: torch.Tensor,\n        # [batch_size]\n        num_rejected_tokens: torch.Tensor,\n        num_tokens: int,\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n        # cu_target_query_lens: [0, a, a + b, a + b + c]\n        # num_rejected_tokens: [n1, n2, n3]\n        # num_tokens_per_req: [a - n1, b - n2, c - n3]\n        # cu_num_tokens: [0, a - n1, a + b - n1 - n2, a + b + c - n1 - n2 - n3]\n        # [a, b, c] -> [a - n1, b - n2, c - n3]\n        num_tokens_per_req = query_len_per_req - num_rejected_tokens\n\n        # [a - n1, b - n2, c - n3] ->\n        # [0, a - n1, a + b - n1 - n2, a + b + c - n1 - n2 - n3]\n        cu_num_tokens = torch.empty_like(cu_target_query_lens)\n        torch.cumsum(num_tokens_per_req, dim=0, out=cu_num_tokens[1:])\n        cu_num_tokens[0] = 0\n\n        token_indices = torch.empty(\n            num_tokens,\n            dtype=torch.int32,\n            device=cu_num_tokens.device,\n        )\n\n        batch_size = num_rejected_tokens.shape[0]\n        BLOCK_SIZE = 1024\n        prepare_eagle_input_kernel[(batch_size, )](\n            token_indices,\n            cu_target_query_lens,\n            cu_num_tokens,\n            BLOCK_SIZE=BLOCK_SIZE,\n        )\n        return cu_num_tokens, token_indices\n\n    def load_model(self, target_model: nn.Module) -> None:\n        draft_model_config = \\\n            self.vllm_config.speculative_config.draft_model_config\n        target_attn_layer_names = set(\n            get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, Attention).keys())\n\n        self.model = get_model(vllm_config=self.vllm_config,\n                               model_config=draft_model_config)\n\n        draft_attn_layer_names = (\n            get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, Attention).keys() -\n            target_attn_layer_names)\n        assert len(draft_attn_layer_names) == 1\n        self.attn_layer_name = next(iter(draft_attn_layer_names))\n\n        # share embed_tokens with the target model if needed\n        if get_pp_group().world_size == 1:\n            logger.info(\n                \"The EAGLE head shares the same vocab embedding\" \\\n                \" with the target model.\"\n            )\n            self.model.model.embed_tokens = target_model.model.embed_tokens\n        else:\n            logger.info(\n                \"Since PP > 1, the EAGLE head loaded its own vocab embedding\" \\\n                \" weights instead of sharing them with the target model.\"\n            )\n\n        # share lm_head with the target model if needed\n        # some model definition do not define lm_head explicitly\n        # and reuse embed_tokens for lm_head, e.g., CohereForCausalLM\n        if self.vllm_config.speculative_config.method != \"eagle3\" and \\\n                hasattr(target_model, \"lm_head\"):\n            logger.info(\"Loading EAGLE LM head weights from the target model.\")\n            self.model.lm_head = target_model.lm_head\n\n    @torch.inference_mode()\n    def dummy_run(\n        self,\n        num_tokens: int,\n    ) -> None:\n        with set_forward_context(None, self.vllm_config,\n                                 num_tokens=num_tokens):\n            self.model(\n                self.input_ids[:num_tokens],\n                self.positions[:num_tokens],\n                self.hidden_states[:num_tokens],\n            )\n\n\n# NOTE(woosuk): Currently, the below code is not used and we always use argmax\n# to sample the draft tokens. We will use this after we find a way to manage\n# the draft prob tensor.\n# Refer to https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/16899 for the details.\n# FIXME(woosuk): The logic here is duplicated with the main sampling code.\n# We should refactor this to reuse the same sampling implementation.\ndef compute_probs_and_sample_next_token(\n    logits: torch.Tensor,\n    sampling_metadata: SamplingMetadata,\n) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n    if sampling_metadata.all_greedy:\n        # For greedy requests, draft_probs is not used in rejection sampling.\n        # Therefore, we can just return the logits.\n        probs = logits\n        next_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)\n        return next_token_ids, probs\n\n    is_greedy = sampling_metadata.temperature == -1\n    temperature = torch.where(is_greedy, 1.0, sampling_metadata.temperature)\n    logits.div_(temperature.view(-1, 1))\n    probs = logits.softmax(dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32)\n\n    # NOTE(woosuk): Currently, we ignore most of the sampling parameters in\n    # generating the draft tokens. We only use the temperature. While this\n    # could degrade the acceptance rate, it does not affect the distribution\n    # of the generated tokens after rejection sampling.\n\n    # TODO(woosuk): Consider seeds.\n    q = torch.empty_like(probs)\n    q.exponential_()\n    # NOTE(woosuk): We shouldn't use `probs.div_(q)` because the draft_probs\n    # will be used later for rejection sampling.\n    next_token_ids = probs.div(q).argmax(dim=-1).view(-1)\n    if not sampling_metadata.all_random:\n        greedy_token_ids = probs.argmax(dim=-1)\n        next_token_ids = torch.where(\n            is_greedy,\n            greedy_token_ids,\n            next_token_ids,\n        )\n    return next_token_ids, probs\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":289,"oldLines":16,"newStart":289,"newLines":13,"lines":[" ","         # [a - n1, b - n2, c - n3] ->","         # [0, a - n1, a + b - n1 - n2, a + b + c - n1 - n2 - n3]","-        cu_num_tokens = torch.empty_like(cu_target_query_lens)","+        cu_num_tokens = torch.zeros_like(cu_target_query_lens)","         torch.cumsum(num_tokens_per_req, dim=0, out=cu_num_tokens[1:])","-        cu_num_tokens[0] = 0","-","         token_indices = torch.empty(","             num_tokens,","             dtype=torch.int32,","-            device=cu_num_tokens.device,","+            device=cu_target_query_lens.device,","         )","-","         batch_size = num_rejected_tokens.shape[0]","         BLOCK_SIZE = 1024","         prepare_eagle_input_kernel[(batch_size, )]("]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
